# Research appendix: ESG Astraa SEO and AEO

*Supporting data for [`esg-astraa-whitepaper-plan.md`](./esg-astraa-whitepaper-plan.md), from the 32-agent workflow run on 1 Oct 2026. The judges and fact-checkers had no live search access, and some research dimensions had little, so treat demand and difficulty tiers as estimates. URLs on github.com are mirrors; replace them with primary sources before citing.*

## 2. Judges' cross-cutting notes

### SEO judge

METHOD LIMITS (read first). I could not spot-check live search results in this run:
- WebSearch refused every query because the session's budget was used up (200 of 200 calls).
- Firecrawl search returned HTTP 402 (out of credits).
- The egress proxy blocked every search engine (Google, Bing, DuckDuckGo, Yahoo, Startpage, Ecosia, Qwant, Yandex, Mojeek, Marginalia, Google Trends), Wikimedia pageviews, and the competitor and regulator sites I tried (thesustainabilitycloud.com and climate-decode.com via WebFetch; sebi.gov.in, pib.gov.in and economictimes.indiatimes.com via curl).
- Only github.com and raw.githubusercontent.com were reachable.

No search volumes, keyword difficulty or SERP features were observed, and none are claimed. Every demand and difficulty statement above is an estimate.

The candidates I am least sure about, and which need a live SERP check first once access returns, are C04, C05, C09, C12, C17, C18, C21, C22, C24, C28, C31 and C45. To unblock: raise CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_WEB_SEARCHES_PER_SESSION, add Firecrawl credits, or allow google.com/bing.com in the environment's Network access settings.

PROXIES USED INSTEAD:
(1) Category SERPs captured earlier in this session by the brand pass (BRSR software, CBAM software, CCTS software, India carbon accounting).
(2) News cadence from a GitHub-archived Economic Times/Moneycontrol digest (https://github.com/abhishekfolder-spec/my-news), 66 daily files from 27 Jul to 1 Oct 2026:
- CBAM appears on 18 of the 66 days, with distinct stories on the FTA CBAM annex, the exporter awareness session, EEPC's supplier-data demand, UK recognition of CCTS, the BRICS opposition, verifier registry access, the WTO dispute and 'carbon cash home'.
- BRSR, SSA 5000, PCAF, SBTi, CSRD and GRI appear on 0 days.
- Caveat: the feed set is markets, economy and top stories, so trade topics are over-represented.
(3) Competitor content visible on GitHub:
- Syntropy Earth's llms.txt shows a small Indian advisory already publishing on BRSR Core assessment vs assurance, CSRD after Omnibus I, and a glossary.
- An EU importer site (Kinzoku) publishes India CBAM default-value content.
- Acquis has an India CBAM cost dashboard, and a third-party JSW actual-vs-default case study exists.
- A Hinglish CA-update site covered SSA 5000 on 21 Sep 2026.
- CCTS-745 (CC BY, created 29 Jul 2026, 0 stars) has no SEO front end.

Regulatory checks I could make first-hand:
- G.S.R. 739(E) and draft G.S.R. 517(E) are MoEFCC notifications, and the steel schedule is still marked DRAFT in the gazette text. Note that the CCTS-745 README wrongly says 'Ministry of Power'; do not copy it.
- UK CBAM from 1 Jan 2027 in five sectors is corroborated by the EU Climate Advisory Board's dataset.
- SSA 5000's effective date (FYs from 1 Apr 2027) comes only from a secondary site.
- Whether the steel CCTS targets have been finalised could NOT be verified.

CROSS-CUTTING OBSERVATIONS (lens: organic opportunity for a young domain):
1. A domain with no backlinks wins first through structured, refreshable data that answers entity-level long-tail queries and earns links. Head terms ('BRSR', 'CBAM India', 'best BRSR software') are infeasible in the near term.
   - The strongest organic engines are C17 (per-entity CCTS pages), C12 (per-factor-family pages), C24 (per-CN-code pages), C18 (per-plant steel lookup) and C01 (per-company BRSR Core pages, which only work if ungated).
   - Commercial guides should then hang off these pillars.
2. Several candidates are really cluster posts, not separate pillars. Consolidate to avoid cannibalisation:
   - BRSR Core assurance pillar: C02 + C03 + C13 (+ C04).
   - CBAM exporter pillar: C24 + C25 + C26 + C30.
   - Interplay pillar: C27 + C28 + C29.
   - CCTS pillar: C17 + C18 + C19 + C20 + C21.
   - Benchmark dataset: C01 + C10 + C05 share one parse.
3. MISSING high-demand, feasible topics:
   - 'BRSR format Excel / template download' and 'BRSR XBRL filing utility how-to' (transactional, seasonal Jun-Sep).
   - 'CBAM communication template for installations explained'.
   - A live 'carbon credit certificate price India' tracker for when CCC trading starts (start date unverified).
   - 'Indian Carbon Market portal registration' how-to (every obligated entity must register).
   - Programmatic '<company> BRSR FY2025-26' pages.
   - Hindi/Hinglish versions for MSME, CCTS-textile and CA audiences (Hinglish coverage already exists for SSA 5000).
4. The off-core topics C38, C39, C43 and C44 would dilute topical authority without realistic ranking prospects.
5. Brand-SERP hygiene comes before any of this. ESG Astraa's own CCTS post states 'nine sectors' against PIB's 'seven' and needs a dated update. Publish every asset with ungated HTML, keyword slugs (not /insights/blogs/2), dated 'last verified' stamps and Organization/Article/FAQ schema, or none of these papers will rank.

### AEO judge

VERIFICATION LIMITS (read first): I could not spot-check live answer engines or SERPs this session. WebSearch returned "budget exhausted (200 of 200)". Firecrawl returned HTTP 402 (out of credits; the user needs to add credits). The egress proxy blocked WebFetch and curl to every non-GitHub host I tried, including google.com, duckduckgo, brave, bing, sebi.gov.in, pib.gov.in, cea.nic.in, eur-lex, competitor sites (thesustainabilitycloud.com, climes.io, relific.io) and Indian news sites. So no live AI answer, PAA box, autocomplete or search volume was observed, and no demand figure below is measured. Where I talk about demand, it is an ESTIMATE from proxies: how often a question recurs across the supplied VoC question list, how crowded the competitor content is, and how much news it gets.

What I could check, via GitHub only:
- (a) Competitor syntropyearth already has a dated (2 Sep 2026) answer-capsule page that correctly answers "is reasonable assurance still mandatory / assessment vs assurance". It also notes that BRSR Section A points 14-15 now name the provider and the type of check. That makes the C01 statistic extractable, but it weakens C02's novelty. https://github.com/syntropyearth/syntropyearth-site/blob/HEAD/thinking/brsr-core-assessment-or-assurance-sebi/index.html
- (b) An open CC BY dataset, CCTS-745, already exists. Its README attributes the gazettes to the "Ministry of Power" and lists draft Phase III (G.S.R. 517(E)) alongside the notified phases. So there is a correctable, citable error, but also a free alternative. https://github.com/prakulhiremath/ccts-745
- (c) India CBAM default values from IR 2026/1740 (EC Excel v2, 6 Aug 2026) are already republished as an open CSV and an MCP tool. Examples: 7601 unwrought aluminium 1.87, 7203 DRI 4.2, and 7207 semi-finished steel 4.27 tCO2e/t, with 10/20/30% mark-ups. https://github.com/Meetv9/ecosetu.cbam/blob/main/data/india_cbam_defaults.csv and https://github.com/Keremozdemirra/cbam-mcp
- (d) A CC0 'Open India Emission Factors' database (v1.3, 5 Aug 2026; 116 factors including 51 CEA V21 grid factors) exists but is labelled AI-extracted and unverified. https://github.com/Creator619-Python/open-india-emission-factors
- (e) Secondary sources say SSA 5000 is effective for financial years beginning on or after 1 Apr 2027 and withdraws SSAE 3000 and SAE 3410 (CA update dated 21 Sep 2026). I did not read the primary ICAI text. https://github.com/SumitGarg100000/Virtual_CA/blob/HEAD/data/updates/icai-issues-ssa-5000-for-sustainability-assurance-aligning-with-global-standards-1789956914439.json
- (f) CBAM is the ESG topic that keeps recurring in Economic Times headlines over 15 Sep to 1 Oct 2026: verifiers getting EU registry access (17 Sep) and the WTO CBAM dispute (28-30 Sep). BRSR did not appear in that feed. https://github.com/abhishekfolder-spec/my-news/tree/HEAD/docs/archive

Model self-check (one model with a June 2026 cutoff; not a prediction for other engines): without retrieval I could answer the March 2025 assessment-or-assurance change. I could NOT give any FY2025-26 filing statistic, facility-level CCTS targets, the steel CCTS draft, SSA 5000, UK recognition of CCTS, the IR 2026/1740 defaults, or confidently the CEA V21 grid factor. Those post-cutoff, India-specific factual gaps are where retrieval-based answers must cite someone.

CROSS-CUTTING:
1. The strongest citation assets are filing- or gazette-derived tables refreshed annually: C01, C05 and C10 should be one 'ESG Astraa India BRSR Index' with chapters, and C17, C18 and C40 should share one CCTS dataset. Put the brand and index name in every stat sentence, given the ghost-citation pattern.
2. Explainer, playbook and framework papers (C03, C06, C13, C14, C34, C38, C44) carry few original numbers and score low on this lens.
3. Platform-telemetry hooks are weak for a pre-scale company with no visible clients. Filing, gazette and EC-data hooks are credible now.
4. Missing candidates:
- a programmatic, versioned 'CEA grid emission factor' page (C12 covers part of this)
- an 'India CBAM trade exposure index' built from Eurostat CN-level imports from India
- a 'how to download and parse BRSR XBRL' reference page, which also seeds C01's credibility

### BUSINESS judge

RESEARCH LIMITS: Live web research was mostly blocked in this run. WebSearch returned "budget exhausted (200 of 200)", Firecrawl returned HTTP 402 (out of credits), and the egress proxy refused every non-GitHub host I tried (32 hosts, including sebi.gov.in, pib.gov.in, eur-lex, economictimes, business-standard, climes.io and crediblesg.com). I worked from primary-text mirrors on GitHub and from ESG Astraa's own public product docs. I verified the following:
(1) PRODUCT FIT EVIDENCE.
- The ideal customer is "Novaferro Steel Ltd": a listed iron and steel maker, about ₹4,000 crore turnover, exporting to the EU, and a CCTS obligated entity (https://raw.githubusercontent.com/singhpratham19/esgastraa-tool/master/docs/00-product.md).
- v1 includes BRSR/BRSR Core with "XBRL-ready export", the full CBAM path from project setup to declaration and verification, CCTS compliance position (no trading), a supplier portal and auditor traceability.
- v1 excludes PCF/LCA beyond the CBAM boundary, ESG ratings submission and CCTS trading. CSRD, SBTi, UAE rules, PCAF, social metrics and physical risk are not mentioned at all (same URL).
- The build order puts CBAM at Milestone 7. Milestone 7b holds the auditor workspace, CCTS Form A and the CCTS-vs-CBAM reconciliation, which the docs call "the most defensible screen in the product" (https://raw.githubusercontent.com/singhpratham19/esgastraa-tool/master/docs/06-build-order.md).
- The competitive-gaps doc names three buyers who own one dataset: the CFO (BRSR), exports/commercial (CBAM) and the plant head (CCTS). It says to lead with CBAM: "most acute pain, least contested budget, weakest incumbents". It also says "no product on either side reconciles" CCTS and CBAM (https://raw.githubusercontent.com/singhpratham19/esgastraa-tool/master/docs/08-competitive-gaps.md).
- The repo shows only documentation plus Milestone-1 status (https://github.com/singhpratham19/esgastraa-tool). The local esgtools repo has no XBRL, CBAM or CCTS code.
- Consequence: every proprietary hook that relies on "platform telemetry", "anonymised client data" or "wizard runs" (C03, C06, C07, C13, C23, C24, C25, C27) is not feasible today. Hooks should use public data (gazettes, CBAM defaults, BRSR filings) plus clearly labelled Novaferro illustrations.
(2) REGULATORY URGENCY VERIFIED.
- Reg (EU) 2025/2083 mirror: certificates are sold in 2027 for 2026 imports; the declaration and surrender are due by 30 Sep of the following year; the quarterly holding requirement drops from 80% to 50%; there is a 50 t de minimis (https://raw.githubusercontent.com/legalize-dev/legalize-eu/main/eu/32025R2083.md).
- Reg 2025/2546 mirror: a physical site visit is required in the first verification year (https://raw.githubusercontent.com/legalize-dev/legalize-eu/main/eu/32025R2546.md).
- The steel GEI schedule G.S.R. 517(E) is explicitly a "DRAFT NOTIFICATION". Rule 6 sets compensation at twice the average CCC price, payable within 90 days (https://github.com/prakulhiremath/ccts-745/blob/main/274012.pdf). Whether the steel draft has been finalised is NOT verified.
- ICAI issued SSA 5000 on about 20 Sep 2026. It is effective for financial years starting on or after 1 Apr 2027, with carve-outs on joint audits and forward-looking statements (Business Standard via https://github.com/lokeshmohanty/pinch-of-salt, data/articles/1649ce822b02829b.json).
- Read via ET headline mirrors (https://github.com/abhishekfolder-spec/my-news, docs/archive/):
  - Indian verifiers gained EU CBAM registry access in Sep 2026 (ET articleshow 134269246)
  - EEPC asked for mandated accredited emission data from raw-material suppliers on 7 Sep 2026 (ET 133830040); the "about 70% MSME" figure is NOT verified
  - the UK recognised CCTS on 8-10 Sep 2026 (ET 133890963)
  - the government held a CBAM awareness session in Aug 2026 (ET 133329985)
- GTRI's 15-22% price-cut estimate was seen only as a secondary citation (https://github.com/CodeWithEugene/INDUX-5.0-2026/blob/main/docs/problem.md).
(3) PATTERN.
- The best fits are the CBAM, CCTS and steel cluster (C26, C27, C18, C17, C24, C25) plus the assurance-evidence spine (C03). These match the "Sell CBAM first" wedge, the steel ideal customer and named product screens. They also reach buyers with budget: CFO, exports head and plant head.
- The BRSR data studies (C01, C05, C10) carry high PR value. But they need an XBRL ingestion pipeline the product does not have, and they target the BRSR "land-and-expand" motion, not the wedge.
- The international, finance-sector, social and physical-risk papers (C14-C16, C32-C44) are off-product and reach buyers who are not ESG Astraa's.
(4) GAPS AND RISKS.
- Missing topics: an aluminium-specific CCTS+CBAM deep dive (16 entities, the smelter boundary issue), and an EU-importer-facing "sourcing from India" brief to win the declarant side.
- A substantiated case study is needed to replace the H&M desk research. The homepage claim of "70% faster BRSR" has no method behind it. Any paper on claims or evidence (C07, C09, C42) exposes ESG Astraa to that inconsistency.
- A small team should combine C26, C24 and C25 into one CBAM flagship, and C18, C17 and C27 into one CCTS/steel flagship, rather than produce six papers.

### TIMING judge

ACCESS LIMITS: WebSearch was exhausted (200/200) on the first call, and Firecrawl returned "insufficient credits". The egress proxy blocked every regulator, news and competitor domain I probed (sebi.gov.in, pib.gov.in, icai.org, eur-lex, gov.uk, ghgprotocol.org, globalreporting.org, thesustainabilitycloud.com, climes.io, sprih.com, syntropyearth.com, google/bing/ddg). Only GitHub was reachable. So all verification below comes from GitHub-hosted mirrors of legal texts and dated news feeds. I could not see live SERP rankings, so white-space judgements rest on the supporting research plus GitHub code searches (for example, a search for CCTS vs CBAM reconciliation content returned only research repos and no vendor pages).

DECISIVE DATES CHECKED (Oct 2026 to early 2028 window):
1) EU CBAM. Article 20 certificate sales apply from 1 Feb 2027 (consolidated Reg 2023/956, Art 36(d): https://raw.githubusercontent.com/legalize-dev/legalize-eu/main/eu/32023R0956.md). The declaration and surrender are due by 30 Sep of the year after import, there is a 50 t de minimis, and the quarterly holding requirement is 50% (Reg 2025/2083 recitals 3, 15 and 27: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/legalize-dev/legalize-eu/main/eu/32025R2083.md). The certificate price is a quarterly average in 2026 and becomes weekly from 2027 (Reg 2025/2548: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/legalize-dev/legalize-eu/main/eu/32025R2548.md). VERIFIED.
2) CBAM default-value mark-ups are 10% for 2026, 20% for 2027 and 30% from 2028 for steel, aluminium, cement and hydrogen. This is quoted from the consolidated IR 2025/2621 as replaced by IR 2026/1740 (OJ 31.7.2026), via a tool README refreshed 24 Sep 2026 (https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Keremozdemirra/cbam-mcp/main/README.md). VERIFIED (secondary quote of the primary text).
3) CBAM verification requires a physical site visit in the first verified year (Reg 2025/2546 recital 3: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/legalize-dev/legalize-eu/main/eu/32025R2546.md). VERIFIED.
4) BRSR Core assessment or assurance applies to the top 1,000 from FY2026-27, after top 500 in FY2025-26 (Syntropy Earth, published 2 Sep 2026, updated 24 Sep 2026, citing the SEBI circulars of 12 Jul 2023 and 28 Mar 2025: https://github.com/syntropyearth/syntropyearth-site/blob/HEAD/thinking/brsr-core-assessment-or-assurance-sebi/index.html). VERIFIED (secondary; sebi.gov.in blocked).
5) ICAI SSA 5000 applies to financial years beginning on or after 1 Apr 2027. It withdraws SSAE 3000 and SAE 3410, with carve-outs on joint audits and forward-looking statements (Business Standard of 20 Sep 2026 via a feed mirror: https://github.com/lokeshmohanty/pinch-of-salt/blob/main/data/articles/1649ce822b02829b.json). A Hindu analysis appeared 23 Sep 2026 (https://github.com/davidrimbell/greenwasher.github.io). ISSA 5000 applies to periods beginning on or after 15 Dec 2026 (https://github.com/youssefbenlallahom/EY_RAG/blob/cfc0d6e479e0e73f3607d2c49912b42c66b9aac3/output/ey-gl-ey-csrd-barometer-05-2025.md). VERIFIED (secondary).
6) CCTS. Under G.S.R. 739(E), compliance years are FY2025-26 (pro-rated from Sep 2025) and FY2026-27. Environmental compensation is twice the average CCC trading price, payable within 90 days. The steel draft is G.S.R. 517(E) (e-gazette 02-07-2026). Source: gazette text (https://github.com/prakulhiremath/CCTS-745). VERIFIED from the gazette text. NOT verified: whether the steel targets have been finalised, and whether CCC trading has actually started. Secondary repos cite CERC CCC Regulations 2026 on indiancarbonmarket.gov.in and a first trading window in Oct 2026 (https://github.com/arrya5/BLARAA-SYSTEMS/blob/main/docs/ComplianceCore-CCTS.md); I could not confirm either.
7) UK CBAM starts 1 Jan 2027 (Finance Act 2026, Royal Assent 18 Mar 2026; SI 2026/802). It has a GBP 50k threshold, a first return due 31 May 2028, and it reaches downstream fasteners (https://github.com/LordPixma/defdr/blob/main/docs/research/14-sector-industrial-logistics-agri.md). Secondary; gov.uk blocked.
8) Other dates checked:
- GRI 102 is effective 1 Jan 2027 (standard text: https://github.com/S-PANDIYAN/Sustainable-Business-Model-AI).
- EmpCo has applied since 27 Sep 2026 (https://github.com/neoki/wealth-engine/blob/main/evidence/eu-green-claims-deadline-2026-09-14.md).
- SBTi V2 was published 11 Jun 2026 and is mandatory after 31 Jan 2028 (https://github.com/Githamza/teamWheelsLP).
- The WTO DSB agreed to establish a panel on Russia's CBAM complaint, and India reserved third-party rights, in late Sep 2026 (https://raw.githubusercontent.com/abhishekfolder-spec/my-news/main/docs/archive/2026-09-29.html).

URGENCY CLUSTER: The heaviest pressure falls between 1 Jan 2027 and 30 Sep 2027:
- CBAM mark-up rises to 20% (1 Jan 2027)
- weekly CBAM pricing begins (2027)
- certificate sales open (1 Feb 2027)
- first CBAM site-visit verifications (H1 2027)
- CCTS FY2026-27 year-end (31 Mar 2027)
- SSA 5000 takes effect (1 Apr 2027)
- the top-1,000 BRSR Core cycle runs through filings (Jun-Sep 2027)
- first CBAM surrender (30 Sep 2027)
Candidates anchored here score highest. Topics whose deadlines fall in 2028-2029 (CSRD for non-EU groups, CSDDD, CBAM downstream, SBTi V2 mandatory, UK CBAM first return) or that have no deadline at all (EDCI, glossary, MACC, PCF) score low on this lens.

WHITE-SPACE OBSERVATIONS:
- Syntropy Earth, a small Bengaluru advisory with llms.txt and Article JSON-LD, already publishes AEO-optimised pages on BRSR Core assessment-or-assurance (C02) and on CSRD Omnibus for non-EU subsidiaries (C16). That narrows white space for both.
- Several 2026 GitHub tools already offer plant-level CCTS GEI lookups (BLARAA, Aangara, carbon-intelligence-india, CCTS-745). A lookup alone is not differentiating; validated lineage plus a CBAM overlay is.
- No vendor CCTS-to-CBAM reconciliation content was found.

MISSING TOPICS WORTH ADDING (time-critical):
(a) A practical guide to the first CCTS true-up: BEE procedure, ACVA verification, CCC issuance and surrender, and the deemed-baseline risk for missing documents. FY2025-26 has closed and FY2026-27 closes 31 Mar 2027.
(b) An 'authorised CBAM declarant and registry readiness' explainer for Indian exporters' EU importers, timed to the Q4 2026 price and the Feb 2027 sales opening.
(c) CEA grid factor v22, expected around Dec 2026, which is a refresh trigger for C12.
Fix ESG Astraa's 'nine sectors' CCTS blog line before publishing any carbon-market paper.

## 4. AEO question bank

Questions buyers ask answer engines, de-duplicated, grouped by the dimension that found them.

### aeo-geo

- What is BRSR Core and which KPIs does it cover?
- What is the difference between BRSR Core assessment and assurance?
- Which listed companies need BRSR Core assessment or assurance in FY2026-27?
- What evidence do auditors ask for in a BRSR Core assurance?
- How much does BRSR Core assurance cost in India?
- How do I calculate GHG emission intensity per rupee of turnover adjusted for PPP in BRSR?
- What is the latest CEA grid emission factor for India?
- How do I calculate Scope 3 emissions for an Indian manufacturing company?
- Are there India-specific spend-based emission factors in INR?
- What emission factor should I use for steel purchased in India?
- What percentage of India's top 1,000 companies disclose Scope 3 emissions?
- What is the average GHG intensity of Indian cement, steel or chemicals companies under BRSR?
- Which companies are obligated entities under India's Carbon Credit Trading Scheme?
- What are the GEI targets for cement or aluminium under CCTS for 2025-26 and 2026-27?
- What happens if an obligated entity misses its CCTS target?
- What will carbon credit certificates cost in India and where are they traded?
- How is CCTS different from the PAT scheme?
- What CBAM default values apply to Indian steel and how much will CBAM cost per tonne?
- Do Indian steel exporters need verified emissions data for CBAM in 2026?
- Does the India-EU FTA exempt Indian exporters from CBAM?
- Does CSRD apply to Indian companies after the Omnibus?
- How do I collect ESG data from MSME suppliers for BRSR value-chain disclosures?
- What is India's green steel taxonomy and star rating?
- What is the best ESG reporting software for Indian manufacturers?
- How does ESG Astraa compare to Watershed, Persefoni, Greenly or Credibl for BRSR?
- What are the cheapest decarbonisation levers for an Indian mid-size manufacturer in rupees per tonne CO2e?

- What is the 2% and 75% rule for BRSR value chain partners?

- How should a company disclose Scope 3 emissions in BRSR if data is not yet available?
- How do I calculate Scope 3 emissions for BRSR as an Indian manufacturer?
- How do I collect ESG data from multiple plants and business units without spreadsheets?
- What is the best BRSR reporting software for mid-cap listed companies in India?
- How should an Indian SME answer different ESG questionnaires from multiple large customers?
- What is the latest CEA grid emission factor for India, and which version should BRSR use?
- Can I use international emission factors (DEFRA, IPCC, EPA) for Indian operations?
- How do I account for electricity from multiple states, captive power and renewable PPAs in Scope 2?
- What should I do when emission factors change between reporting periods?
- Which electricity emission factor should an Indian plant use for CBAM indirect emissions?
- Do Indian exporters have to file CBAM declarations, or does the EU importer file?
- What emissions data will my EU customer ask for under CBAM in 2026?
- I run an installation in India: how do I share CBAM emissions data with my EU declarant?
- What happens under CBAM if my raw-material or precursor supplier cannot provide actual emissions data?
- What are CBAM default values, and how do they compare with actual verified data for Indian steel?
- Who can verify CBAM emissions data in India, and do I need an accredited verifier?
- When is the first CBAM annual declaration and certificate surrender due for 2026 imports?
- Can carbon prices paid under India's CCTS be deducted from the EU CBAM obligation?
- Does the UK CBAM recognise India's Carbon Credit Trading Scheme, and what does it mean for exporters?
- What does the India-EU FTA say about CBAM and carbon prices paid in India?
- How much will CBAM cost Indian steel and aluminium exporters?
- What documents (mill test certificates, heat numbers) do EU steel buyers need for CBAM?
- Can the same CBAM emissions data be used for multiple shipments?
- Does the CBAM 50-tonne threshold exempt small EU importers of Indian goods?
- Will the WTO dispute over CBAM delay or stop CBAM obligations for Indian exporters?
- Is my company or plant an obligated entity under India's Carbon Credit Trading Scheme (CCTS)?
- Is the steel sector covered under CCTS, and what are the GHG emission intensity targets for 2026-27?
- How is the CCTS greenhouse gas emission intensity (GEI) target calculated?
- What happens if an obligated entity misses its CCTS GEI target?
- How does the PAT scheme transition to CCTS for designated consumers?

### sectors-india

- Is the iron and steel sector covered by India's Carbon Credit Trading Scheme, and is the target final or still draft?
- Which steel plants have GHG emission intensity targets under CCTS, and what is my plant's 2026-27 target?
- What happens if an obligated entity misses its CCTS GEI target or fails to submit verification documents?
- How is a CCTS GHG emission intensity figure different from CBAM specific embedded emissions and from BRSR Scope 1+2 intensity?
- Do Indian forgings, castings, fasteners or steel pipes need CBAM emissions data in 2026?
- What emissions data will my EU customer ask for under CBAM, and when do certificate purchases start?
- Which textile mills are covered by CCTS, and what does a spinning or processing unit need to measure?
- How should an Indian real estate developer calculate embodied carbon for cement and steel?
- Can CCTS gazette baselines be used as supplier-specific emission factors for Scope 3?
- What is India's grid emission factor for FY2024-25 and which version should BRSR use?
- Does BRSR require Scope 3 emissions, and what does BRSR Core assurance cover in FY2026-27?
- How many entities are obligated under CCTS and in which sectors?
- What is India's green steel taxonomy threshold and how does certification work?
- What do global pharma buyers and the NHS require from Indian API suppliers on carbon reporting?
- Which Indian sectors are most exposed to CBAM, CCTS and BRSR at the same time?

### emerging-themes

- Is BRSR Core assurance mandatory for the top 1,000 listed companies in FY2026-27, and what is the difference between assessment and assurance?
- What will an assurer test under SSAE 3000 or ISSA 5000 when assuring BRSR Core data?
- How do we build internal controls over sustainability reporting for BRSR, CCTS and CBAM?
- Can AI draft our BRSR, and how do we make AI-generated ESG data audit-ready?
- What are India's CCPA greenwashing guidelines and what evidence do they require for 'eco-friendly' claims?
- Does the EU Empowering Consumers Directive apply to Indian exporters selling into the EU from 27 September 2026?
- Is the EU Green Claims Directive still happening?
- Can we call a product 'carbon neutral' in the EU if we use carbon offsets?
- What are the UK CMA's rules for suppliers whose data is used in a retailer's green claims?
- What internal carbon price should an Indian steel or aluminium exporter use given CBAM and CCTS?
- How much will CBAM cost per tonne of exported steel in 2026-2027?
- How should Indian companies report market-based Scope 2 using green open access, RECs or I-RECs?
- Will the GHG Protocol Scope 2 revision (hourly matching) affect Indian companies' renewable energy claims?
- What is the Renewable Consumption Obligation and who are designated consumers?
- Do CCTS obligated entities have to report Scope 2 emissions?
- How do we assess physical climate risk such as heat stress and water stress for Indian manufacturing plants?
- What must employers do during heatwaves in India under the 2026 advisories?
- How should a company disclose operations in water-stressed areas in BRSR?
- How do India's new Labour Codes change BRSR Principle 3 disclosures?
- What human rights due diligence do Indian suppliers need for EU customers under CSDDD after the Omnibus?
- Which EPR rules apply to Indian manufacturers in 2026 (plastic, e-waste, battery, tyres, ELV, C&D, non-ferrous scrap)?
- Should Indian companies report on TNFD or nature-related risks yet?
- What is the energy and water footprint of AI, and should it be disclosed in ESG reports?
- Which Indian ESG software keeps an audit trail and evidence for each data point?

### whitepaper-playbook

- Which Indian listed companies need BRSR Core assurance in FY2026-27?
- What is the difference between assurance and assessment under SEBI's BRSR Core?
- Who are the main BRSR Core assurance providers in India?
- How many top-1000 Indian companies obtained reasonable assurance on BRSR Core?
- Where can I download BRSR XBRL files for Indian listed companies?
- Is Scope 3 reporting mandatory in BRSR?
- What are the BRSR value-chain disclosure thresholds after SEBI's March 2025 circular?
- How is Scope 1 and 2 emission intensity per rupee of turnover adjusted for PPP calculated?
- What is a typical GHG emission intensity for an Indian cement, steel or pharma company?
- What is the CBAM default value for Indian aluminium or steel?
- What mark-up applies to CBAM default values in 2026, 2027 and 2028?
- How much will CBAM cost an Indian steel exporter in 2027?
- Is it cheaper to report actual embedded emissions than to use CBAM default values?
- When do CBAM certificate sales start and when is the first CBAM declaration due?
- Which companies are covered under India's Carbon Credit Trading Scheme?
- Is the iron and steel sector covered under CCTS GHG emission intensity targets?
- Can BRSR data be reused for CBAM and CCTS reporting?
- Which Indian companies face BRSR, CCTS and CBAM at the same time?
- How much does BRSR reporting and assurance cost in India?
- What is the best software for BRSR, CBAM and CCTS compliance in India?

### international-markets

- Does the UAE climate law (Federal Decree-Law No. 11 of 2024) require private companies to report emissions, and by when?
- What is the reporting threshold and deadline for Abu Dhabi facility-level GHG MRV?
- What are the fines for not reporting emissions in the UAE?
- Is ESG reporting mandatory for Saudi listed companies?
- When does SGX require Scope 3 emissions, and which companies are deferred to FY2028 or FY2030?

- When do Indonesia's PSPK 1 and PSPK 2 become mandatory?
- Is IFRS S2 mandatory in Sri Lanka or Bangladesh?

- Can an Indian supplier refuse CSRD data requests from EU customers (the value-chain cap)?
- What is the difference between EU CBAM and UK CBAM for Indian steel exporters?
- Does the India-UK CETA exempt Indian exporters from UK CBAM?

- How can one emissions dataset serve BRSR, CBAM, CSRD and UK SRS at the same time?
- What carbon data will EU fashion brands ask Bangladeshi or Indian garment factories for?

- What is the VSME standard, and should non-EU suppliers use it?

## 5. Key findings by research dimension

### aeo-geo (96 searches run)

- **[high]** VERIFICATION LIMIT: WebFetch was blocked by the egress proxy for searchenginejournal.com, semrush.com, ahrefs.com, tryprofound.com, seerinteractive.com, arxiv.org, developers.google.com and sebi.gov.in, and Firecrawl had no credits. So every finding below comes from WebSearch result summaries of the named pages, not from reading the pages myself. Treat the numbers as reported figures and re-check them against the primary page before quoting them in a white paper. ([source](https://developers.google.com/search/docs/fundamentals/ai-optimization-guide), 2026-10-01)
- **[high]** Google's official position (guide published 15 May 2026, 'Optimizing your website for generative AI features on Google Search'): AI Overviews and AI Mode are 'rooted in our core Search ranking and quality systems'. Optimising for them is 'still SEO'. They use RAG and query fan-out over the Search index. You do not need to chunk content, add special schema, or create AI text files such as llms.txt. ([source](https://www.searchenginejournal.com/googles-new-ai-search-guide-calls-aeo-and-geo-still-seo/575026/), 2026-05)
- **[medium]** Ranking in the top 10 matters less for AI Overview citations than it used to. In Ahrefs' March 2026 study (863k keywords, ~4M AIO citations), only 38% of cited pages ranked in the organic top 10 for the same query, down from 76% in its July 2025 study. About 31% ranked 11-100 and about 31% ranked below 100. Fan-out sub-queries pull in pages that rank for related questions. ([source](https://news.designrush.com/ai-overview-citations-drop-ahrefs), 2026-03)
- **[medium]** BrightEdge (Feb 2025 to Feb 2026): the share of B2B Technology queries showing an AI Overview rose from 36% to 82%. Overlap between AIO citations and the B2B-tech top 10 is in the low 20s%. In practice, most B2B buyer queries now get an AI answer that cites pages beyond page one. ([source](https://almcorp.com/blog/google-ai-overviews-surge-9-industries/), 2026-02)
- **[medium]** Seer Interactive (Sep 2025; 3,119 informational queries, 42 organisations): on queries with an AI Overview, organic CTR fell 61% (1.76% to 0.61%) and paid CTR fell 68%. Brands cited inside the AIO earned 35% more organic clicks and 91% more paid clicks than uncited brands. Being cited is now how you protect clicks. ([source](https://www.seerinteractive.com/insights/aio-impact-on-google-ctr-2026-update), 2025-09 / 2026 update)
- **[medium]** Scale: at Google I/O 2026, AI Overviews were reported at over 2.5B monthly users and AI Mode at over 1B monthly users. AI Mode queries are about 3x longer than classic search queries (Kevin Indig, H1 2026). ([source](https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/sundar-pichai-io-2026/), 2026-05)
- **[high]** India context: Google rolled out AI Mode to everyone in India on 8 July 2025 and added Hindi in Sep 2025. Sam Altman said India has 100M weekly ChatGPT users and is OpenAI's second-largest market (Feb 2026). Indian ESG buyers are already searching through answer engines. ([source](https://blog.google/intl/en-in/feed/ai-mode-in-google-search-rolling-out-in-india/), 2025-07 / 2026-02)
- **[high]** B2B buying behaviour (G2 survey of 1,076 B2B software buyers, March 2026; published 15 Apr 2026): 51% now start software research with an AI chatbot more often than with Google, up from 36% seven months earlier. 69% chose a different vendor than planned because of chatbot guidance. ([source](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/new-g2-research-half-of-b2b-software-buyers-now-start-their-research-with-ai-chatbots-302742807.html), 2026-04)
- **[medium]** How ChatGPT picks citations (Ahrefs, 1.4M prompts): only about 50% of the URLs ChatGPT retrieves end up cited. The strongest predictor is how semantically close the page title is to ChatGPT's hidden fan-out sub-queries (0.602 for cited pages vs 0.484 for uncited). Natural-language URL slugs were cited 89.78% of the time vs 81.11% for other slugs. Reddit pages make up 67.8% of retrieved-but-uncited URLs and are cited only 1.93% of the time. ([source](https://ahrefs.com/blog/why-chatgpt-cites-pages/), 2026)
- **[low]** ChatGPT's link to Bing is weakening. In 2024 Seer found 87% of SearchGPT citations matched Bing's top 10. Later 2026 reporting (attributed to Profound) says Bing alignment fell while Google alignment rose, so ranking in both Bing and Google matters. I could not verify the Profound numbers. ([source](https://www.seerinteractive.com/insights/87-percent-of-searchgpt-citations-match-bings-top-results), 2024-2026)
- **[medium]** Platforms behave differently. Semrush (230k+ prompts over 13 weeks): Perplexity's citations overlap Google's top 10 by about 91% at domain level and 82% at URL level, vs 53.68% and 35.41% for Google AI Mode. Reddit and LinkedIn are top-5 cited domains on ChatGPT, AI Mode and Perplexity. ([source](https://www.semrush.com/blog/most-cited-domains-ai/), 2025-2026)
- **[medium]** Citations rarely carry across engines. Profound's study of 680M citations (Aug 2024 to Jun 2025) found only about 11% of domains are cited by both ChatGPT and Perplexity. Kevin Indig's H1 2026 report says 91% of citations appear in only one of ChatGPT, Perplexity or AI Overviews. A white paper has to be promoted through several channels, not tuned for a single engine. ([source](https://www.tryprofound.com/blog/ai-platform-citation-patterns), 2025-06 / 2026-07)
- **[medium]** Claude: Anthropic lists Brave Search as its web-search subprocessor (added 19 Mar 2025). Third-party analyses report that 79-87% of Claude-cited URLs are in Brave's top 10. Anthropic runs three separate robots.txt user agents: ClaudeBot (training), Claude-SearchBot (search index) and Claude-User (live fetch). Blocking one does not block the others. The overlap figures are third-party estimates. ([source](https://www.searchenginejournal.com/anthropics-claude-bots-make-robots-txt-decisions-more-granular/568253/), 2025-2026)
- **[high]** Microsoft Copilot answers are grounded in Bing's index. On 27 Feb 2026 Bing rewrote its Webmaster Guidelines to name GEO and treat 'grounding results and citations' as an eligibility outcome of their own. Bing Webmaster Tools added a free AI Performance report (public preview, 9 Feb 2026) that shows Copilot citations by URL and grounding query. It is the only first-party AI citation dashboard. Microsoft recommends IndexNow so updated pages are fetched quickly. ([source](https://blogs.bing.com/webmaster/February-2026/Introducing-AI-Performance-in-Bing-Webmaster-Tools-Public-Preview), 2026-02)
- **[medium]** Ghost citations (Semrush with Kevin Indig, June 2026; 3,981 domain appearances, 115 prompts, 14 countries): about 62% of AI citations cite a domain without naming the brand. ChatGPT cites sources about 87% of the time but names brands in only about 20.7% of appearances. Gemini names brands in about 83.7% of appearances but cites only about 21.4%. White-paper statistics should carry the brand and index name in the sentence itself. ([source](https://searchengineland.com/ghost-citation-problem-ai-483794), 2026-06)
- **[high]** The GEO paper (Aggarwal et al., KDD 2024, arXiv:2311.09735) tested content changes on GEO-bench and found they can raise visibility in generative-engine answers by up to 40%. The best methods were Cite Sources, Quotation Addition and Statistics Addition. Keyword stuffing did not help. ([source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.09735v2), 2023-11 (KDD 2024))
- **[medium]** Answer capsules: a Search Engine Land audit (15 domains, about 2M monthly sessions) found 72.4% of ChatGPT-cited blog posts had a self-contained answer of 40-60 words directly under the title or a question-style H2. About 91% of those capsules had no links. Capsule plus original data (34.3% of cited posts) was the strongest combination. ([source](https://searchengineland.com/how-to-get-cited-by-chatgpt-the-content-traits-llms-quote-most-464868), 2025-2026)
- **[medium]** Position on the page matters. Kevin Indig's analysis of about 1.2M ChatGPT answers found 44.2% of citations come from the first 30% of a page's text (a 'ski ramp'). Put the headline numbers and definitions at the top of each white-paper page, not in a closing chapter. ([source](https://searchengineland.com/chatgpt-citations-content-study-469483), 2026)
- **[medium]** Original data only gets cited when it is formatted as a benchmark. In a sample of 301 AI-cited pages, only 8 were primary research. Those 8 earned 8.4% of all citations, 3.3x the citation density of other pages. Narrative research reports were rarely cited. Named, measurable comparisons ('which is best / how do sectors compare') were. Annual white papers should therefore be built as ranked or comparative benchmark tables. ([source](https://searchengineland.com/why-most-original-data-never-gets-cited-481676), 2026)
- **[medium]** Schema does not cause citations. Ahrefs tracked 1,885 pages that added JSON-LD (Aug 2025 to Mar 2026) against about 4,000 controls. Changes were +2.4% on AI Mode and +2.2% on ChatGPT (both not significant) and -4.6% on AI Overviews (significant). Keep Article, Organization and Dataset markup for entity clarity and Dataset Search, but do not plan around schema as a citation lever. ([source](https://ahrefs.com/blog/schema-ai-citations/), 2026-05)
- **[medium]** FAQ rich results: Google stopped showing them on 7 May 2026, and the Search Console report and API data were removed by Aug 2026. FAQPage is still valid schema.org markup and costs nothing to keep on visible Q&A blocks. Do not expect a Google SERP feature from it. ([source](https://www.techwyse.com/news/ai-search/google-faq-rich-results-deprecated-2026), 2026-05)
- **[high]** There is no evidence llms.txt helps. Google's John Mueller said 'no AI system currently uses llms.txt', and Gary Illyes said Google will not support it (July 2025). SE Ranking found 10.13% adoption across 300k domains and no correlation with AI citations. Adding the file costs little, but it should not count as a tactic. ([source](https://searchengineland.com/google-says-normal-seo-works-for-ranking-in-ai-overviews-and-llms-txt-wont-be-used-459422), 2025-07 / 2026)
- **[medium]** Freshness: Ahrefs' study of about 17M citations across ChatGPT, Perplexity, Gemini, Copilot and AIO found AI-cited pages are about 25.7% fresher than Google organic results. ChatGPT-cited URLs were 393-458 days newer. Publish white papers as versioned HTML with visible 'last updated' dates and refresh them quarterly. Figures such as '+47% from adding a Last-updated date' come only from aggregators and are unverified. ([source](https://ahrefs.com/blog/top-10-most-cited-domains-ai-assistants), 2025-2026)
- **[medium]** Brand mentions beat links. In Ahrefs' 75k-brand correlation study, branded web mentions correlated with AI Overview visibility at r=0.664 and backlinks at r=0.218. YouTube mentions were the strongest single correlate (r about 0.737). This is correlation, not causation, but it supports earning coverage in Indian business media and YouTube for each report. ([source](https://blckalpaca.at/en/knowledge-base/seo-geo/geo-generative-engine-optimization/brand-mentions-vs-backlinks-the-ahrefs-75k-brand-study), 2025-2026)
- **[medium]** Most-cited domains: YouTube is the most-cited domain in AI Overviews at about 22.9% (Ahrefs Brand Radar, Sep 2026), but only about 1.8% in ChatGPT. LinkedIn is reported as the most-cited domain for professional queries, having more than doubled its citation frequency between Nov 2025 and Feb 2026 (Profound). Wikipedia and Reddit together exceed 25% of US ChatGPT citations (5W). For B2B ESG, LinkedIn (company page plus executive articles) and YouTube walkthroughs are the highest-leverage third-party channels. ([source](https://ahrefs.com/blog/most-cited-domains-ai-overviews/), 2026-09)
- **[medium]** Self-ranking listicles backfire. Scrunch (about 10k cited URLs across 7 AI surfaces, May-June 2026; published 24 Sep 2026) found self-ranked vendors were recommended only slightly more often (4% to 7%). A Lily Ray / Search Engine Land study found that when a brand's own 'best X' listicle was cited by AI Overviews, the brand was left out of the recommendation 69% of the time. Avoid self-ranked 'best ESG software India' pages and publish transparent, methodology-led benchmarks instead. ([source](https://searchengineland.com/google-ai-overviews-cite-self-serving-listicles-recommend-competitors-480573), 2026-09)
- **[low]** Review sites act as a gate for inclusion more than as a cited source. One 2026 study of 40 B2B SaaS categories found G2 and Capterra almost never appear as ChatGPT citations, yet nearly all recommended tools had G2 and Capterra reviews. In AI Overviews, G2 took about 23.1% of review-platform citations. ESG Astraa needs complete G2 and Capterra India profiles to qualify for shortlists. These figures come from vendor and agency studies. ([source](https://siteup.ai/blog/g2-llm-b2b-software-evaluation), 2026)
- **[medium]** Gated and PDF-only white papers are invisible to retrieval. AI crawlers (OAI-SearchBot, PerplexityBot, Claude-SearchBot, Googlebot) cannot submit lead forms. Google's guidance says to keep important content in textual HTML form. Publish the full report as ungated HTML (one URL per chapter, with chart data as HTML tables) and offer the designed PDF and CSV as optional downloads. The specific claims about PDF crawl frequency come from secondary sources. ([source](https://ziptie.ai/blog/gated-content-and-ai-search/), 2026)
- **[high]** Crawler access needs checking. Since 1 July 2025 Cloudflare blocks AI crawlers by default for new zones, and it expanded default blocking in Sep 2026. Make sure the CDN/WAF allows OAI-SearchBot, ChatGPT-User, PerplexityBot, Claude-SearchBot, Claude-User, Bingbot and Googlebot. Training bots (GPTBot, ClaudeBot) can be handled separately. ([source](https://searchengineland.com/cloudflare-to-block-ai-crawlers-by-default-with-new-pay-per-crawl-initiative-457708), 2025-07 / 2026-09)
- **[medium]** AI referral traffic converts well but is small. Semrush (500+ B2B topics) reports AI-search visitors convert at 4.4x the rate of organic visitors. Conductor reports AI referrals at about 1.08% of total traffic. Track chatgpt.com, perplexity.ai, copilot.microsoft.com and gemini.google.com as a separate GA4 channel, and use Bing AI Performance plus a fixed prompt panel to measure white-paper citations. ([source](https://www.airops.com/blog/ai-referral-traffic-conversion-rates), 2026)
- **[low]** Entity building: Organization schema with sameAs links to LinkedIn, Crunchbase, G2 and Wikidata is the standard way to disambiguate a brand. A Wikidata item needs notability (independent references), so get press coverage first. Vendor claims of a ~20% AIO visibility lift from entity linking (Schema App) are unverified. ([source](https://www.stackmatix.com/blog/organization-schema-knowledge-graph), 2026)
- **[high]** SERP test, 'what is BRSR Core': results are spread across consultancies and vendors (Schneider Perspectives, Uniqus, Benchmark Gensuite, Taxmann, India Briefing, Glocert, Treeni, Orennow, SYNE). No SEBI page appeared in the top results, even though the primary document is SEBI's Annexure I PDF. No single source dominates, which leaves room for a definitive, well-sourced HTML answer. ([source](https://perspectives.se.com/blog-stream/embracing-the-brsr-core-framework), 2026-10 (SERP observed))
- **[high]** SERP test, 'best ESG reporting software India': results are directories (SourceForge, Capterra India) plus vendor self-listicles (thesustainabilitycloud.com held 3 slots, samcorporate, sparrowrms, zoeiesg, 4seer). The global competitors (Watershed, Persefoni, Greenly, Credibl) did not appear. There is no independent India comparison with a published methodology. ([source](https://sourceforge.net/software/esg/india/), 2026-10 (SERP observed))
- **[high]** SERP test, 'how to calculate scope 3 emissions for Indian manufacturers': results are the GHG Protocol PDF, UNFCCC, Normative and generic vendor blogs. None has India-specific factors. Follow-up searches for INR-denominated spend-based (EEIO) factors and for an Indian per-tonne Category 1 steel factor returned no authoritative source. Only an open GitHub 'open-india-emission-factors' project and CEA/GHG Platform India data exist. This is the clearest gap for an India-specific, well-structured answer. ([source](https://ditchcarbon.com/blog/the-big-guide-to-scope-3-emission-factors-2025-what-to-use-when-and-where-to-get-them), 2026-10 (SERP observed))
- **[high]** Extractable formats win on simple factual queries. For 'CEA grid emission factor 2024-25', small vendor programmatic pages (batchwise.ai 'Latest Value, 5-Year Trend', carbonneeti, reclimatize) rank alongside or above the primary CEA V21.0 PDF. They put the value in the title and present tables. The primary figure is CEA V21.0 (Dec 2025): FY2024-25 weighted average emission factor of 0.710 tCO2/MWh. ([source](https://cea.nic.in/wp-content/uploads/baseline/2025/12/User_Guide_V_21.0.pdf), 2025-12)
- **[high]** Data-led BRSR benchmarks come mostly from academia and think tanks, published as PDFs or gated. Examples: IIM Bangalore (982 FY2024-25 filings; Aug 2026), CEEW (1,258 filings on air emissions; 16 Jul 2026), NSE-CFA Institute (300 companies; Oct 2024), IIM Ahmedabad (Feb 2025), XBRL International (Feb 2024), and StepChange BRSR Barometer 2025 (800+ companies; download appears gated). No ESG software vendor runs an ungated, HTML-first annual India benchmark with downloadable sector tables. ([source](https://www.businesstoday.in/india/story/top-1000-listed-indian-companies-see-53-jump-in-fy25-carbon-emissions-iim-bangalore-study-552106-2026-08-30), 2024-2026)
- **[high]** Example of an extractable statistic that earned media pickup. IIM Bangalore found 57.1% of top-1,000 listed companies did not disclose Scope 3 in FY2024-25, and Scope 1+2 emissions rose 4.29% year on year, with 53.1% of companies reporting increases. The single stat-led headline was picked up by BusinessToday. This is the pattern ESG Astraa should copy. ([source](https://www.businesstoday.in/india/story/top-1000-listed-indian-companies-see-53-jump-in-fy25-carbon-emissions-iim-bangalore-study-552106-2026-08-30), 2026-08)
- **[high]** BRSR data quality is a quotable, measurable gap. CEEW (Jul 2026) found particulate matter reported in more than a dozen incompatible units across BRSR filings. NSE-CFA flagged unclear data sources and inconsistent units. Running ESG Astraa's validation checks over public BRSR XBRL filings would produce a new 'data-quality / assurance-readiness' metric that nobody else publishes. ([source](https://www.ceew.in/publications/air-emission-reporting-brsr-esg-frameworks), 2026-07)
- **[medium]** Regulatory why-now, BRSR Core. SEBI circular SEBI/HO/CFD/CFD-PoD-1/P/CIR/2025/42 (28 Mar 2025) replaced 'reasonable assurance' with 'assessment or assurance' and extends the glide path to the top 1,000 listed entities from FY2026-27. Value-chain ESG disclosure is voluntary for the top 250 from FY2025-26 and covers partners with at least 2% of purchases or sales, capped at 75% coverage. I could not fetch sebi.gov.in directly; this is confirmed only through secondary sources. ([source](https://vinodkothari.com/2025/04/brsr-disclosures-for-value-chain-partners-eased-by-sebi/), 2025-03)
- **[medium]** Regulatory why-now, assurance standard. ICAI has issued the Standard on Sustainability Assurance SSA 5000, reportedly effective from 1 Apr 2027 (BusinessToday, Sep 2026). Assurance-readiness content will have a second trigger in 2027. I have not seen the effective date in a primary ICAI notification. ([source](https://www.businesstoday.in/india/story/icai-working-out-assurance-standards-for-sustainability-reporting-by-india-inc-556674-2026-09-20), 2026-09)
- **[medium]** Regulatory why-now, CCTS (India's carbon market). The GHG Emission Intensity Target Rules were notified 8 Oct 2025 (282 obligated entities in aluminium, cement, chlor-alkali and pulp and paper). A Jan 2026 amendment added 208 units (refineries, petrochemicals, textiles, secondary aluminium). Targets apply to 2025-26 and 2026-27 against an FY2023-24 baseline. A shortfall triggers environmental compensation of 2x the average CCC trading price, levied by CPCB. ([source](https://icapcarbonaction.com/en/news/india-notifies-emission-intensity-targets-nine-sectors-under-carbon-credit-trading-scheme), 2025-10 / 2026-01)
- **[medium]** Regulatory why-now, CCC trading. CERC notified its Carbon Credit Certificate trading regulations on 27 Feb 2026 (gazette 3 Mar 2026). Trading is on IEX, PXIL and HPX in monthly sessions within a floor and forbearance price band, with first trading expected around Oct 2026. The actual floor and forbearance values were not found. Published price expectations (₹250-1,500/t) are analyst estimates only. ([source](https://solarquarter.com/2026/03/03/cerc-notifies-2026-regulations-to-formalize-indias-carbon-credit-trading-market/), 2026-03)
- **[medium]** Regulatory why-now, CBAM. The definitive period began 1 Jan 2026. A 50-tonne annual de minimis threshold now applies (Omnibus Regulation (EU) 2025/2083). Certificate sales start 1 Feb 2027. The EU-India FTA concluded in Jan 2026 gives India no CBAM exemption, only a 'technical dialogue'. Vendor blogs quote very inconsistent India default values and costs (e.g. about 2.5 tCO2/t, or €250+/t vs €65-170/t with actual data); these are unverified, which is why an authoritative CBAM benchmark would be cited. ([source](https://www.argusmedia.com/en/news-and-insights/latest-market-news/2781007-eu-india-fta-leaves-cbam-untouched), 2026-01)
- **[medium]** Regulatory why-now, CSRD. Omnibus I Directive (EU) 2026/470 was published 26 Feb 2026 and entered into force 18 Mar 2026. Non-EU parents, including Indian groups, are in scope only above €450M EU net turnover plus an EU subsidiary or branch above €200M, from FY2028 with first reports in 2029. India-relevant CSRD content should be about value-chain data requests from EU customers rather than direct obligations. ([source](https://www.globalpolicywatch.com/2026/02/eu-csddd-csrd-omnibus-published-in-official-journal-transposition-delegated-acts-and-guidelines-are-next/), 2026-02)
- **[high]** India-specific benchmark anchor for steel. The Ministry of Steel's Green Steel Taxonomy (12 Dec 2024) defines green steel as below 2.2 tCO2e per tonne of finished steel, with 5/4/3-star bands at below 1.6, 1.6-2.0 and 2.0-2.2. It covers Scope 1, Scope 2 and limited Scope 3, with NISST as the MRV agency. This lets ESG Astraa publish India-anchored comparisons for steel buyers (Scope 3 Category 1) and exporters (CBAM). ([source](https://www.pib.gov.in/PressReleaseIframePage.aspx?PRID=2083839), 2024-12)
- **[medium]** Under-served question types: (1) India-specific emission factors (spend-based INR, materials, regional grid) with versioning; (2) sector benchmarks for BRSR intensity metrics (GHG per ₹ crore PPP-adjusted, water KL per ₹ crore); (3) the cost and evidence requirements of BRSR Core assessment vs assurance (only vendor cost guesses such as ₹15-25 lakh); (4) CCTS GEI targets by sector in sortable form, plus exposure to CCC prices; (5) CBAM default vs actual values for Indian production routes; (6) MSME supplier data readiness. A practitioner guide also observes that Perplexity mixes US/UK references into BRSR answers, a sign that well-structured India-context sources are scarce (low confidence). ([source](https://coraa.ai/university/ai-lab/tool-guides/perplexity-for-audit), 2026-10 (SERP synthesis))
- **[medium]** WHITE-PAPER TACTIC CHECKLIST, drawn from the evidence above. (1) Publish ungated HTML first, one URL per chapter with a natural-language slug and a question-style H1/H2 that mirrors fan-out sub-queries. (2) Start each section with a link-free answer capsule of 40-60 words and keep key stats in the first 30% of the page. (3) Present findings as named, ranked benchmarks (sector medians, quartiles, 'which sector is best/worst') rather than narrative. (4) Write each stat as a self-contained sentence with the brand and index name and the primary source (SEBI, CEA, BEE, MoEFCC). (5) Publish the methodology, sample size and data dates, plus CSV downloads with Dataset schema. (6) Show a visible 'last updated' date, refresh quarterly, ping IndexNow, and monitor Bing AI Performance. (7) Seed LinkedIn (company page and executive articles), a YouTube walkthrough and Indian business-media coverage. (8) Use Organization/Article schema for entity clarity, not as a citation lever. Skip llms.txt and self-ranked listicles. ([source](https://searchengineland.com/why-most-original-data-never-gets-cited-481676), 2026)

### india-brsr (59 searches run)

- **[high]** ACCESS LIMITS. WebFetch was blocked by the egress proxy for every domain tried (sebi.gov.in, thefootnotes.in, syntropyearth.com, kpmg.com, taxguru.in). Firecrawl returned 'insufficient credits' (402). The shared WebSearch budget ran out after 59 searches. So every fact below comes from search-engine result snippets and AI summaries of the cited pages, not from reading the full pages. SEBI primary documents (circulars, FAQs) were seen only through exchange re-circulations (NSE, BSE, MSEI) and law-firm or consultancy summaries. ([source](https://www.sebi.gov.in/sebi_data/faqfiles/apr-2025/1745399101865.pdf), 2026-10-01 session limitation)
- **[high]** BRSR is mandatory for the top 1,000 listed entities by market capitalisation. The BRSR goes to the exchange as both PDF and XBRL on the same day as the annual report; there is no separate XBRL deadline. Other listed entities, including SME-exchange companies, may file voluntarily. ([source](https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/web/sites/default/files/inline-files/Business%20Responsibility%20and%20Sustainability%20Report%20FAQs%20&%20General%20Observations%20%20Guidelines%20for%20filing%20of%20BRSR.pdf), NSE/BSE FAQ May 2024; still the operative filing guidance as far as found)
- **[high]** SEBI circular SEBI/HO/CFD/CFD-PoD-1/P/CIR/2025/42 of 28 March 2025 replaced mandatory 'reasonable assurance' of BRSR Core with 'assessment or assurance'. Assessment means a third-party assessment under standards developed by the Industry Standards Forum (ISF: ASSOCHAM, CII, FICCI) in consultation with SEBI. NSE and BSE re-circulated it on 1 April 2025. ([source](https://ca2013.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/NSE-Circular_01.04.2025.pdf), 28 Mar 2025 circular; exchange notices 1 Apr 2025)
- **[medium]** Provider rules under the March 2025 framework. The assessment or assurance provider need not be a Chartered Accountant (profession-agnostic), and the listed entity's Board must ensure the provider has the necessary expertise. There is also a conflict-of-interest bar: the provider and its associates must not sell products or provide non-assurance services, including consulting, to the listed entity or its group. Implication for ESG Astraa: as advisory plus software vendor, it cannot also act as BRSR Core assessor for its own clients, so it should position as the readiness and evidence partner. ([source](https://ca2013.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/BSE-Circular_01.04.2025.pdf), Apr 2025; wording taken from search summaries of the BSE notice and Vinod Kothari FAQs)
- **[high]** BRSR Core assessment/assurance glide path by market-cap rank: top 150 in FY2023-24, top 250 in FY2024-25, top 500 in FY2025-26, top 1,000 in FY2026-27. That means about 500 companies (ranks 501-1,000) get BRSR Core assessed or assured for the first time on FY2026-27 data, which is being generated now. ([source](https://www.sebi.gov.in/sebi_data/meetingfiles/dec-2024/1735040682024_1.pdf), SEBI board memo Dec 2024; confirmed by 2026 secondary sources (thefootnotes.in, csquarecarbon.com))
- **[high]** Value-chain ESG disclosures: - Apply to the top 250 listed entities on a voluntary basis from FY2025-26. They were deferred by one year and moved from comply-or-explain to voluntary. - Assessment or assurance of value-chain disclosures is voluntary from FY2026-27. - The value chain means upstream and downstream partners that individually account for 2% or more of purchases or sales by value. Disclosure may be limited to partners covering 75% of purchases or sales. - Prior-year figures are voluntary in the first year. ([source](https://www.teamleaseregtech.com/updates/article/40959/sebi-issued-a-circular-regarding-the-measures-to-facilitate-ease-of-do/), 28 Mar 2025 circular; SEBI board decision 18 Dec 2024)
- **[high]** The same March 2025 circular added a voluntary leadership indicator under BRSR Principle 6: Green Credits generated or procured by the listed entity and by its top ten value-chain partners (by value of purchases and sales). It applies to BRSRs for FY2024-25 onwards. ([source](https://www.business-standard.com/markets/news/sebi-includes-green-credit-program-under-brsr-framework-125032801374_1.html), 28 Mar 2025)
- **[medium]** The ISF 'Industry Standards on Reporting of BRSR Core' were notified through SEBI circular SEBI/HO/CFD/CFD-PoD-1/P/CIR/2024/177 dated 20 Dec 2024 and apply from FY2024-25. They include methodology for PPP-adjusted revenue intensity using IMF PPP conversion rates. I could not confirm whether the ISF has published a separate, dated 'standard on third-party assessment'. Sources say the ISF 'will develop' one or refer to it generically. ([source](https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/web/sites/default/files/inline-files/NSE_Circular_20122024-%20SEBI%20Circular_0.pdf), Dec 2024; ISF assessment-standard status unverified as of 2026-10-01)
- **[medium]** Assurance standards currently cited as acceptable for BRSR Core: ISAE 3000, ISSA 5000, or ICAI's SSAE 3000 and SAE 3410. ([source](https://vinodkothari.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/01/BRSR-FAQs-25012025-2.pdf), 2025)
- **[high]** Around 20 Sept 2026, ICAI issued SSA 5000 (Standard on Sustainability Assurance) with a General Requirements & Framework for Sustainability Assurance Engagements. - In force for financial years beginning on or after 1 April 2027, i.e. FY2027-28. - SSAE 3000 and SAE 3410 will be withdrawn. - Based on IAASB's ISSA 5000 with carve-outs on joint audits and forward-looking statements. - Prepared in discussion with SEBI. ([source](https://www.business-standard.com/industry/news/icai-issues-new-standard-on-sustainability-assurance-effective-april-2027-126092000336_1.html), 20 Sep 2026 (11 days before research date))
- **[high]** ICAI's Sustainability Reporting Standards Board published an Exposure Draft of a Framework for Sustainability Assurance Engagements on 20 May 2026, with comments due 19 June 2026. This was the precursor to SSA 5000. ([source](https://www.icai.org/post/srsb-exp-draft-framework-20052026), May-Jun 2026)
- **[medium]** Today's SERP for SSA 5000 is almost entirely news wires (Business Standard, ANI, Tribune, BusinessToday, plus a Hinglish Whalesbook piece). I found no company-side practical explainer. This is a low-competition, newly created query space (estimate; proxy: SERP composition). ([source](https://www.whalesbook.com/news/Hinglish/sebiexchange/ICAI-Unveils-SSA-5000-Sustainability-Assurance-Standard-for-FY28/6aafaa6f32997ce1de8a6fff), Sep 2026)
- **[medium]** KPMG India (Feb 2026) analysed 94 NIFTY 100 companies' FY2024-25 BRSRs: - All obtained reasonable assurance on BRSR Core. - 57 extended verification beyond the regulatory scope. - 61 reported standalone and 32 consolidated. - Nearly half restated FY24 comparatives, 16 of them to align with the Dec 2024 ISF standards. - One construction company received a qualified opinion. ([source](https://assets.kpmg.com/content/dam/kpmgsites/in/pdf/2026/02/chapter-1-emerging-trends-in-brsr-reporting-by-listed-companies.pdf.coredownload.pdf), Feb 2026; covers FY2024-25 data)
- **[medium]** A review of NIFTY 100 FY2024-25 filings reportedly found that no company used the new ISF 'assessment' route; all chose reasonable assurance. The same source says FY2025-26 reports show Big Four affiliates, SGS, DNV, BDO, S.R. Batliboi and smaller firms as providers. ([source](https://thefootnotes.in/articles/brsr-core-assurance-fy2026-27-checklist), 2026 (via search summary))
- **[medium]** The latest large-sample BRSR studies all cover FY2024-25 or earlier. IIM Bangalore's 2026 report covers 982 of the top 1,000 companies (FY2024-25). CFA Institute's 'Current State of BRSR in Corporate India 2.0' (June 2026) covers 300 companies over FY2022-23 to FY2024-25. I found no published analysis of FY2025-26 filings: the first year the top 500 needed assessment or assurance and the first voluntary value-chain year. This is a clear data-content gap. ([source](https://www.iimb.ac.in/sites/default/files/inline-files/ESG%20_BR-2026.pdf), 2026; absence of FY2025-26 study as of 2026-10-01 (based on searches only))
- **[medium]** FY2025-26 BRSRs are being filed now, with annual reports ahead of AGMs (typically by 30 Sept). Company-level news shows FY26 BRSRs filed with reasonable assurance on BRSR Core (e.g. Reliance Power, Data Patterns). NSE's BRSR filings page offers XBRL/CSV downloads, which makes a proprietary filing dataset feasible. ([source](https://www.nseindia.com/companies-listing/corporate-filings-bussiness-sustainabilitiy-reports), Jul-Sep 2026)
- **[high]** ICRA ESG Ratings (27 Jan 2026): among NSE top 200 companies, the number disclosing Scope 3 rose 58.7% over four years (75 to 119), and reported Scope 3 emissions rose 119%. Upstream categories dominate. Sector Scope 3 intensity changes: autos -10%, cement +67%, metals & mining +32%. ([source](https://www.icraesgratings.in/Content/assets/pdf/Press%20Release%20-%20Scope%203%20Research%20-%20ICRA%20ESG%20Ratings.pdf), 27 Jan 2026)
- **[high]** SEBI ESG Rating Provider (ERP) rules: - Master Circular for ERPs issued 11 July 2025. - April 2025 tweaks: subscriber-pays ERPs must share reports with subscribers and the rated issuer simultaneously, with two working days for issuer comments. - SEBI set up a Working Group to review the ERP framework (Press Release 13/2026, 18 Feb 2026). Its recommendations were not found published as of 2026-10-01. ([source](https://www.business-standard.com/markets/news/sebi-forms-working-group-to-review-esg-rating-norms-enhance-transparency-126021801229_1.html), Jul 2025; Feb 2026)
- **[medium]** SEBI's ESG mutual fund rules (circular of 20 July 2023): - ESG schemes must invest at least 65% of AUM in companies that report comprehensive BRSR and have assurance on BRSR Core. - Compliance from 1 Oct 2024, with legacy schemes given until 30 Sept 2025. - There are six ESG strategy categories, and at least 80% of assets must follow the stated strategy. NOT VERIFIED: whether the mutual fund rule text was updated to 'assessment or assurance' after March 2025. ([source](https://www.skylinerta.com/pdf_file/51_671021621_SEBICircular-ESG-DisclosurebyMutualFund-20.07.2023.pdf), Jul 2023 rule; status of 2025 alignment unverified)
- **[high]** SEBI framework for ESG debt securities (circular of 5 June 2025): covers social, sustainability and sustainability-linked bonds (green bonds are under separate rules). It requires alignment with recognised standards (e.g. ICMA), pre- and post-issuance disclosures, and third-party review to curb 'purpose-washing'. ([source](https://www.azbpartners.com/bank/sebis-framework-for-esg-debt-securities/), 5 Jun 2025)
- **[high]** CCPA notified the Guidelines for Prevention and Regulation of Greenwashing or Misleading Environmental Claims on 15 Oct 2024. Claims must be specific and backed by credible certification or scientific evidence, and cherry-picking is prohibited. These rules are relevant to marketing claims such as unsubstantiated performance figures. ([source](https://www.pib.gov.in/PressReleasePage.aspx?PRID=2064963&reg=48&lang=2), 15 Oct 2024)
- **[medium]** No 2026 SEBI circular amending BRSR or BRSR Core was found in searches. Policy pressure points: - BRSR is described as about 40% aligned with ISSB/IFRS S2 (ORF). - IEEFA proposed a two-phase roadmap to add climate transition-plan disclosures to BRSR. - Green Central Banking (21 Sep 2026) links BRSR disclosure quality to transition finance. BRSR is not mandatory for unlisted companies. An MCA committee proposal to extend it remains unimplemented as far as found. ([source](https://www.orfonline.org/research/enhancing-the-interoperability-of-india-s-business-sustainability-reporting-with-international-standards), 2025-Sep 2026; sebi.gov.in could not be checked directly)
- **[medium]** OUTDATED OR WRONG CONTENT IN RANKING PAGES (based on snippets and AI summaries, not full page reads): - relific.io's FY2026-27 article is titled 'India's Reasonable Assurance Mandate' and describes all nine attributes as requiring reasonable assurance, although assessment has been an alternative since March 2025. - irqs.co.in's comparison page reportedly says assessment 'is not considered as formal validation under BRSR', which contradicts the circular. - The esg360.in post of Jan 2026 is summarised as saying value-chain assessment becomes 'mandatory' in FY2026-27; it is voluntary. - Search summaries also surfaced claims that BRSR Core assurance must come from ICAI members, and that BRSR Core applies to the top 250 in FY2026-27. Both are wrong. ([source](https://relific.io/blogs/brsr-core-fy-2026-27-india-s-reasonable-assurance-mandate-explained-for-listed-companies), 2026)
- **[medium]** Stale filing guidance still ranks. The NSE/BSE BRSR filing FAQs (May 2024) say a 'Reasonable Assurance Certificate' must be attached, which pre-dates the March 2025 'assessment or assurance' change. Some 2026 guides still give the BRSR deadline as 'by September 30, 2025'. ([source](https://ca2013.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/BSE-FAQ_13.05.2024.pdf), 2024 FAQ still surfacing in 2026)
- **[high]** The SERP for 'best BRSR reporting software India 2026' is led by vendor listicles carrying stale year labels: The Sustainability Cloud's 'Best BRSR Software in 2025' and Passionate in Marketing's '...in 2025'. Others ranking are Oren's buyer's guide, ZeroCarbon, Noa/Newtral, Breathe ESG and Sentra. ESG Astraa does not appear. ([source](https://www.thesustainabilitycloud.com/blog/top-5-brsr-softwares/), SERP observed 2026-10-01)
- **[medium]** Online PPP-intensity guidance is inconsistent. ISF methodology uses IMF PPP conversion rates, yet one search summary of ranking content said SEBI requires the 'annual average exchange rate'. KPMG found restatements to align with the ISF standards. Together these point to an underserved, technical long-tail (estimate; proxy: conflicting snippets and the restatement frequency). ([source](https://assets.kpmg.com/content/dam/kpmgsites/in/pdf/2025/01/firstnotes-sebi-introduces-certain-key-changes-in-brsr-reporting.pdf), Jan 2025 / 2026 SERP)
- **[high]** The nine BRSR Core attributes per SEBI Annexure I: 1. GHG footprint 2. Water footprint 3. Energy footprint 4. Embracing circularity (waste) 5. Employee wellbeing and safety 6. Gender diversity 7. Inclusive development 8. Fairness in engaging with customers and suppliers 9. Open-ness of business At least one AI search summary of ranking pages listed them incorrectly (e.g. 'Training & Skill Development'). This is an AEO accuracy gap that a definitive, schema-marked page can fill. ([source](https://www.sebi.gov.in/sebi_data/commondocs/jul-2023/Annexure_I-Format-of-BRSR-Core_p.pdf), Jul 2023 annexure (still the base format))
- **[medium]** Several CCTS-regulated plants belong to companies that also file BRSR, and CBAM exposure adds a third emissions regime. CEEW and an SSRN paper describe the intensity-based CCTS versus absolute embedded-emissions CBAM mismatch. No operational guide to reconciling BRSR, CCTS and CBAM data was found ranking. ([source](https://www.ceew.in/gfc/publications/Corporate-Emissions-Disclosures), 2025-2026)
- **[high]** KEY DEADLINES THAT CREATE DEMAND: - FY2026-27 (1 Apr 2026 to 31 Mar 2027): top 1,000 need BRSR Core assessment or assurance, about 500 for the first time; top 250 may voluntarily get value-chain disclosures assessed or assured. Reports are due with annual reports before AGMs, typically by 30 Sep 2027. - FY2027-28 (from 1 Apr 2027): SSA 5000 replaces SSAE 3000 and SAE 3410 for Indian sustainability assurance. - Running in parallel: CBAM's first annual declaration and surrender for 2026 imports (by 30 Sep 2027) and CCTS GEI target compliance for 2025-26 and 2026-27 (from the brand context sources). ([source](https://www.business-standard.com/industry/news/icai-issues-new-standard-on-sustainability-assurance-effective-april-2027-126092000336_1.html), Compiled 2026-10-01)

### india-carbon-market (7 searches run)

- **[high]** RESEARCH ACCESS LIMITS (read first). WebSearch returned 'budget exhausted (200/200)' and Firecrawl returned HTTP 402 (no credits), so no fresh SERP, People Also Ask or autocomplete data was collected in this session. The egress proxy blocked pib.gov.in, beeindia.gov.in, cercind.gov.in, moef.gov.in, egazette.gov.in, icapcarbonaction.com, iexindia.com, carbonneeti.com, climate-decode.com, wikipedia, zenodo, arxiv, indiankanoon and prsindia. Only github.com was reachable. The primary regulatory facts below come from official Gazette of India PDFs mirrored in a public GitHub dataset repo, which I downloaded and parsed myself. The 7 'searches' counted are GitHub repository searches. Items marked 'not verified' could not be checked against a primary source. ([source](https://github.com/prakulhiremath/CCTS-745), 2026-10-01)
- **[high]** Phase I targets are final. MoEFCC notified the Greenhouse Gases Emission Intensity Target Rules, 2025 as G.S.R. 739(E) on 8 Oct 2025, under sections 3, 6 and 25 of the Environment (Protection) Act 1986. The draft was G.S.R. 234(E) of 16 Apr 2025. The rules cover four sectors: aluminium, cement, chlor-alkali, and pulp & paper. Baseline year is FY2023-24. Compliance years are FY2025-26 and FY2026-27. Targets are set per obligated entity in tCO2e per tonne of equivalent product. The FY2025-26 targets are pro-rated for Sept 2025 to Mar 2026. My count of registration IDs gives 282 entities: aluminium 13, cement 186 (131 clinker/integrated plants and 55 grinding units), chlor-alkali 30, pulp & paper 53. ([source](https://github.com/prakulhiremath/CCTS-745/blob/main/266804-Publication%20of%20Greenhouse%20Gases%20Emission%20Intensity%20Target%20Rules%2C%202025%20notification%20under%20Carbon%20Credit%20Trading%20Scheme.pdf), Gazette dated 2025-10-08)
- **[high]** Phase II targets are final. The GHG Emission Intensity Target (Amendment) Rules, 2025 were notified as G.S.R. 25(E), dated 13 Jan 2026 (e-gazette ID CG-DL-E-16012026-269375; the draft was dated 23 Jun 2025). They add a Second Schedule with 208 entities: secondary aluminium 3, petroleum refinery 21, petrochemical crackers 11, textiles 173. Refinery targets use tCO2e per MBBLS per NRGF. FY2025-26 targets are pro-rated for Jan to Mar 2026. Phase I and II together cover 490 entities in 7 sectors. That matches PIB's 'nearly 490 obligated entities across seven energy-intensive sectors'. It contradicts ESG Astraa's blog line 'nine hard-to-abate sectors', which needs correcting. ([source](https://github.com/prakulhiremath/CCTS-745/blob/main/269375-Greenhouse%20Gases%20Emission%20Intensity%20Target%20Amendment%20Rules%202025%20notification%20under%20Carbon%20Credit%20Trading%20Scheme%202023.pdf), Gazette dated 2026-01-13)
- **[high]** Iron & steel targets are in draft. MoEFCC published draft G.S.R. 517(E) on 26 Jun 2026 (uploaded 2 Jul 2026). It adds a Third Schedule of 255 iron & steel entities with FY2023-24 baselines and a FY2026-27 target only (no FY2025-26 target), and opened a 60-day objection window. Comments went to ccts.hsm-moefcc@gov.in. NOT VERIFIED: whether the final rules had been notified by 1 Oct 2026. A secondary tracker updated 19 Sep 2026 still lists steel as draft. ([source](https://github.com/prakulhiremath/CCTS-745/blob/main/274012.pdf), Draft dated 2026-06-26; final status unverified at 2026-10-01)
- **[medium]** Fertiliser had no CCTS target. Fertiliser is one of the nine sectors originally named for CCTS, yet it has no target in any of the three gazette documents. The June 2026 draft's preamble lists only the earlier notifications for 4 + 4 sectors. At least one public 'CCTS intelligence' tool says 'Textiles and Fertilisers Now Official' and 'all nine CCTS sectors now have official or near-official targets'. Both statements are wrong according to the gazette. Misinformation of this kind gives an authoritative, gazette-linked tracker an opening. ([source](https://github.com/sneha-tatiwala/carbon-intelligence-india/blob/main/data/ccts-alerts.js), Gazette check as of the 2026-06-26 draft; a later fertiliser notification cannot be ruled out)
- **[high]** Obligations and penalties (verbatim mechanics from G.S.R. 739(E)). Obligated entities must: - register on the Indian Carbon Market portal - submit documents as the BEE detailed procedure requires - surrender banked or purchased Carbon Credit Certificates (CCCs) equal to any shortfall  CCCs issued = (target GEI − achieved GEI) × equivalent output. CCCs to buy = (achieved − target) × output. CCCs can be banked.  If an entity fails to submit documents, its achieved GEI is deemed equal to its baseline GEI when the shortfall is calculated.  If it fails to comply, CPCB imposes environmental compensation of twice the average CCC trading price in that compliance year's trading cycle. BEE sets the average price. The compensation is payable within 90 days; non-payment brings EPA penalties.  GEI targets are recommended by MoP on the advice of BEE and the National Steering Committee for Indian Carbon Market (NSCICM), and notified by MoEFCC. BEE issues CCCs. ([source](https://github.com/prakulhiremath/CCTS-745/blob/main/266804-Publication%20of%20Greenhouse%20Gases%20Emission%20Intensity%20Target%20Rules%2C%202025%20notification%20under%20Carbon%20Credit%20Trading%20Scheme.pdf), 2025-10-08)
- **[medium]** My own calculation from the three gazette schedules (all 745 entities parsed). Method: FY2023-24 output × baseline GEI, at constant output. Refineries use throughput × NRGF. Two textile rows were corrected. - Covered baseline emissions: about 839 MtCO2e (Phase I 359 Mt, Phase II 121 Mt, steel draft 359 Mt). - 'Do-nothing' CCC shortfall at the FY2026-27 targets: about 36.7 Mt (Phase I 12.1, Phase II 4.6, steel 20.0). - 310 of 745 entities face a do-nothing shortfall under 10,000 tCO2e, and 209 under 5,000 t. The median entity shortfall is about 13,700 t. - Median shortfall by segment: textiles about 2,900 t; cement grinding units about 2,000 t; pulp & paper about 6,700 t. - The top 75 entities (10%) hold about 65% of the total shortfall. - 320 entities have no FY2025-26 target (all 255 steel units and 65 others). - Odisha holds about 17.7% of baseline emissions, Chhattisgarh 12.4% and Gujarat 11.5%. - 106 of the 255 steel units produced under 100,000 t in FY2023-24. These are estimates, not official figures. ([source](https://github.com/prakulhiremath/CCTS-745), Computed 2026-10-01 from gazettes dated 2025-10-08, 2026-01-13, 2026-06-26)
- **[high]** The secondary data has errors. The CCTS-745 manuscript reports a 767.60 MtCO2e baseline and a 34.22 Mt FY2026-27 obligation. It also misdates the notifications (for example, Phase I as '19 December 2023' and Phase III as '20 August 2024'), attributes them to the Ministry of Power instead of MoEFCC, and treats the steel draft as notified.
